1.  **Analyze the Check:** The White Knight on e7 attacks g8. The King must move.
2.  **Evaluate `g8h8`:** Moving to h8 places the King in the corner. While it escapes the Knight's check, it might be vulnerable to future mating nets (e.g., involving the Queen on d4 and the h-file). It is generally passive.
3.  **Evaluate `g8g7`:** Moving to g7 places the King on the 7th rank, behind the pawn shield (assuming pawns on f7 and h7 protect it). 4.  **Evaluate `g8f8`:** Moving to f8 is a standard response to escape check from a Knight on e7. It keeps the King on the back rank (rank 8), which is generally safer than the 7th rank. It also avoids the corner constraints of h8. The Queen on d4 does not attack f8 directly, and the Knight does not attack f8. This seems like the most solid and principled move.

Given the choice, `g8f8` is the most standard and safe move to escape the check while maintaining King safety on the back rank.
